Abstract: Linear Independent Component Analysis (ICA) recovers jointly independent source signals from their linear mixtures. To achieve this, classical ICA algorithms attempt to maximize non-Gaussianity, measured by negentropy, which is linked to independence by information theory. Because exact negentropy optimization is intractable, they rely on proxy contrast functions, such as fourth-order cumulants, and parametric log-likelihoods. We propose instead to measure non-Gaussianity using the squared Wasserstein distance $W_2^2$ to a standard Gaussian. We prove that the Wasserstein distance between a standard normal distribution and linear projections of the data is maximized when the projection recovers an independent component. Based on this observation, we propose the OT-ICA algorithm which finds this projection by gradient-based optimization. Empirical evaluation on simulated data shows that OT-ICA outperforms proxy-based methods for different distributions of the latent variables. Application to EEG artifact removal and econometric price discovery confirm OT-ICA can be used for applied ICA tasks without distributional assumptions.

</details>


### [154] [Leveraging unlabelled data for generalizable neural population decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14086)
*Ximeng Mao,Nanda H. Krishna,Avery Hee-Woon Ryoo,Matthew G. Perich,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOJO框架，通过联合掩码自编码（SSL）和监督学习来训练尖峰令牌化神经解码器，在多个物种和任务上超越纯监督学习模型，尤其在数据标注稀缺场景下表现优异，并能泛化至其他神经模态（如ECoG）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于尖峰令牌化的神经解码器仅支持监督学习（SL），限制了模型只能在标注数据上训练，无法利用大量无标注数据。作者旨在引入自监督学习（SSL）以突破这一限制，提升数据利用效率和模型泛化能力。

Method: MOJO采用联合训练框架，结合掩码自编码（SSL目标）和监督学习目标，对尖峰令牌化模型进行端到端训练。在多个数据集上进行评估，包括猴运动皮层尖峰数据、小鼠多脑区视觉/决策数据以及人类ECoG语音数据。

Result: MOJO在猴子运动皮层、小鼠多脑区及人类ECoG数据集上均超越纯监督学习模型；在少样本微调场景下提升尤为显著；学习到的神经元表征更具可解释性，能提升脑区分类和尖峰统计预测性能；可泛化至ECoG连续信号，性能接近专为连续信号设计的神经基础模型。

Conclusion: 为尖峰令牌化模型引入SSL可提升标注稀缺场景的性能，支持利用无标注数据跨任务、跨物种训练，并能推广至其他神经模态，为训练更灵活、可扩展的神经基础模型提供了新路径。

Abstract: Robust and accurate neural decoders are integral to neurotechnologies such as brain-computer interfaces and closed-loop experiments. Recent work has shown that tokenizing neural data at the spike level facilitates multi-session pretraining and delivers state-of-the-art decoding performance. However, current spike-based models are restricted to supervised learning (SL), limiting training to datasets with paired behavioural labels. To address this limitation, we introduce MOJO (Masked autOencoder-based JOint training), a training framework for spike-tokenizing models that jointly leverages self-supervised learning (SSL) via masked autoencoding and SL objectives. We evaluate MOJO on three spiking datasets spanning monkey motor cortex during reaching tasks and multi-regional mouse recordings during vision and decision making tasks, demonstrating superior performance over purely SL-trained models. This improvement is especially pronounced when training with limited labelled data, particularly in few-shot finetuning, where only a small amount of labelled data from a new session is available. Incorporating SSL also yields more interpretable neuronal representations, improving performance on brain region classification and spike-statistics prediction without explicit optimization for these tasks. We further show that MOJO generalizes beyond spiking data to human electrocorticography during speech, where it continues to outperform purely SL-trained models and achieves performance comparable to neuro-foundation models (NFMs) designed specifically for continuous signals. Overall, augmenting spike-tokenizing models with SSL improves performance in label-impoverished settings and enables the use of unlabelled data across various tasks and species, while generalizing to other neural modalities. These results suggest a path towards more flexible and scalable data usage when training NFMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Microflow: Microarchitectural Causal Observability for Deep Cross-Layer Analysis and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13184)
*Saber Ganjisaffar,Chengyu Song,Nael Abu-Ghazaleh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Microflow是一个新型可观测性框架，通过将执行轨迹转化为中间表示（MFIR），显式捕获软件语义、指令、微架构事件和硬件资源之间的因果关系，实现从观察到性能瓶颈到根本原因的系统性追溯，在SPEC CPU 2017基准测试中揭示了传统工具无法发现的隐藏瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有架构模拟器只能提供聚合指标或原始轨迹，无法揭示微架构事件之间的复杂交互及其与程序执行的关系，导致架构师观察到性能症状却无法系统性地将其归因到跨抽象层的根本原因。

Method: 将执行轨迹转化为Microflow中间表示（MFIR），统一捕获软件语义、指令、微架构事件和硬件资源间的依赖关系，通过反事实分析实现精确的停顿归因和关键路径分解。

Result: 在SPEC CPU 2017的leela和mcf基准测试中，成功发现了传统聚合指标无法揭示的隐藏瓶颈：leela中的隐藏分支误预测开销，以及mcf中的跨循环迭代资源竞争。

Conclusion: Microflow将因果关系提升为一等分析对象，使因果性可查询，为性能分析和硬件-软件协同设计提供了坚实基础，能够系统性地推理现有工具无法察觉的复杂硬件-软件交互。

Abstract: Existing architectural simulators expose aggregate metrics or raw traces, but fail to reveal complex interactions among microarchitectural events and their relationship to program execution. Consequently, architects observe performance symptoms but cannot systematically attribute them to root causes across abstraction layers. This paper introduces Microflow, an observability framework elevating causality to a first-class analytical object. Microflow transforms execution traces into the Microflow Intermediate Representation (MFIR), explicitly capturing dependencies across software semantics, instructions, microarchitectural events, and hardware resources. By unifying these elements, MFIR enables direct traversal from observed stalls to their underlying causes, paving the way for automated root-cause analysis. Microflow precisely attributes stalls, reveals unobservable phenomena, and enables exact critical-path decomposition through counterfactual analysis. These capabilities allow systematic reasoning about complex hardware-software interactions opaque to existing tools. Making causality queryable, Microflow provides a strong foundation for performance analysis and hardware-software co-design. We demonstrate it on two SPEC CPU 2017 benchmarks, uncovering bottlenecks invisible from aggregate symptoms: hidden misprediction costs in leela and cross-loop-iteration contention in mcf.

</details>


### [156] [The Economics of AI Decoding Chips: Rebalancing Compute, Capacity, and Bandwidth for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13068)
*Michael J. Yuan,Ju Long*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文指出现有GPU架构"计算密集、内存不足"的特性不适用于大语言模型解码，并提出了一种专门优化的推理加速器HTX-301，通过减少计算单元、增加廉价DDR5内存、降低带宽来大幅降低成本，在DeepSeek-R1 671B模型上实现了比H100集群更优的性价比，且供应链不受任何限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU（如H100）在设计上偏向计算密集型任务，配备大量浮点运算单元但内存容量有限。然而LLM解码阶段计算需求低、内存需求高，导致GPU利用率仅个位数百分比。同时，高带宽内存（HBM）、CoWoS封装等稀缺资源限制了产能和成本，使得大规模部署成本极高。

Method: 提出两个关键常数形式化分析效率瓶颈：F/B（屋顶线脊点）决定计算能否被充分利用；F/S（每GB内存捆绑的计算量）决定必须购买多少计算资源。基于此设计专用解码加速器HTX-301，采用28nm工艺、PCIe接口、商用DDR5内存，大幅降低计算能力、采用更低更便宜的带宽，通过硬件线性扩展支持并发。

Result: 单卡8芯片板卡可容纳DeepSeek-R1 671B，成本约$19,000；4U服务器（4板卡16芯片）服务2个用户，每人20.3 tok/s，成本约$28,000。8台4U服务器支持16个并发用户，总成本$224,000，每百万token成本约$12，而8GPU节点成本约$350,000，每百万token成本$21。

Conclusion: HTX-301证明了通过重新平衡计算-内存配比，可以显著降低LLM推理部署成本。其决定性优势在于不使用HBM、CoWoS和先进制程逻辑芯片，供应链无任何受限输入，可实现大规模低成本部署。

Abstract: Every mainstream GPU is built compute-heavy and capacity-light: it pairs enormous arithmetic throughput with too little memory to hold a modern model. In contrast, large language model decoding requires little compute and a large amount of memory: a GPU's floating-point units run at single-digit-percent utilization during decoding, and the memory the workload does need is sold only bundled with yet more compute. The compute is recovered only at hyperscale, where Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are spread across 96--320-GPU expert-parallel clusters serving thousands of concurrent users, a scale available to a handful of operators. We formalize the inefficiency with two fixed per-chip constants. F/B, the roofline ridge point, determines whether the compute can be utilized; F/S, the compute bundled with each GB of memory, determines how much compute must be bought. We then argue for a rebalanced decode accelerator: less compute, far more commodity memory, and a deliberately lower and cheaper bandwidth. The Skymizer HTX-301, a purpose-built 28nm PCIe accelerator using commodity DDR5, occupies that design point. Its entry cost is low. A single eight-chip card holds DeepSeek-R1 671B for about \$19,000, and a 4U server of four four-chip cards serves two users at a deterministic 20.3 tokens per second each for about \$28,000. Either costs less than a single H100, while the minimum GPU deployment for the model is an eight-GPU node near \$350,000. Concurrency then scales out by adding hardware: eight 4U servers carry sixteen users for about \$224,000, two-thirds of the node's price, with the cost per token unchanged at about \$12 per million against the node's \$21. The HTX-301's decisive advantage is a supply chain free of every rationed input: it uses no high-bandwidth memory, no CoWoS, and no leading-edge logic.

</details>


### [157] [ChipVerilog: A Large-Scale OpenCores-Derived Benchmark for LLM-Based Verilog RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13079)
*Yan Tan,Jiping Du,Xiangchen Meng,Yangdi Lyu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ChipVerilog是一个面向大规模、层次化、跨模块的Verilog RTL生成基准测试，包含64个来自OpenCores的IP/内核设计目标，规模远大于现有模块级基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型Verilog生成基准多基于短小、独立的模块级任务，无法充分反映实际IP和处理器内核RTL中的代码规模、层次结构和模块交互。

Method: 从OpenCores收集OR1200、FPU等五个设计家族的64个生成目标（含单模块和跨模块），从规格文档和参考RTL提取功能并撰写自然语言描述，经人工审核后通过编译、等价性检查或仿真验证。

Result: 大规模RTL生成仍有挑战，尤其是层次化和跨模块设计场景。

Conclusion: ChipVerilog揭示了现有LLM在复杂、大规模RTL生成上的不足，为该领域提供了更具现实意义的新基准。

Abstract: Large language models have shown strong potential for Verilog RTL generation. However, many existing benchmarks are built from short, self-contained module-level tasks. These tasks are useful for controlled evaluation, but they do not fully capture the code scale, hierarchy, and module interactions found in practical IP and processor-core RTL. We present ChipVerilog, a description-to-Verilog generation benchmark built from OpenCores IP/core designs. The benchmark contains 64 generation targets from five design families: OR1200, double-precision FPU, MIPS-16, I2C, and CORDIC. It includes both single-module targets and cross-module targets that instantiate or interact with other RTL modules. Several targets exceed 1,000 lines of Verilog, making ChipVerilog substantially larger and structurally more complex than typical module-level suites. Each benchmark instance is constructed from a pair of specification documents and reference RTL. We extract the target functionality, write a detailed natural-language description, and manually review the description for correctness and clarity. Generated RTL is checked by compilation and validated through equivalence checking for local modules, or by simulation for integrated IP/core targets. Results show that large-scale RTL remains challenging, especially for hierarchical and cross-module designs.

</details>


### [158] [Full-Pipeline Inference Optimization for MiMo-V2.5 Series: Pushing Hybrid SWA Efficiency to the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13095)
*Xiaomi MiMo Team,Anqi Liu,Aoxin Ma,Bo Chen,Bo Yang,Chen Wang,Chen Zhang,Chengda Tang,Chengwei Wang,Chiheng Lou,Depeng Yan,Fuli Luo,Gang Wang,Hailin Zhang,Jiale Sun,Kang Zhou,Rui Huang,Shaohui Liu,Shen Huang,Shijie Cao,Shuaishuai Fan,Tianling Zhou,Xiangwei Deng,Xueyang Xie,Xuli Wang,Yingchun Lai,Yu Yang,Yuan Zhang,Zhen Tang,Zhonghua Deng,Zihan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对MiMo-V2.5模型族的全流水线推理优化方案，结合混合滑动窗口注意力（Hybrid SWA）、稀疏混合专家模型（MoE）和多模态编码器，通过KVCache系统优化（层级预取、SWA感知前缀缓存树、专用放置策略）、GCache高性能分布式缓存基础设施（RDMA优化网络）、KVCache亲和性路由器、多模态输入优化（GPU图像预处理、并行视频解码、多模态缓存共享）等一系列工程手段，首次在生产环境中实现了Hybrid SWA+MoE+多模态复合架构的大规模LLM服务系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hybrid SWA虽然理论上能显著减少注意力计算和KVCache存储，但在实际生产中实现这些收益需要大量工程优化工作，现有系统尚未有效覆盖Hybrid SWA+MoE+多模态这一复合架构。

Method: 1) KVCache系统优化：层级预取、SWA感知前缀缓存树、专用放置策略，实现严格O(W)存储和高缓存命中率；2) 构建GCache分布式缓存基础设施，采用RDMA优化网络；3) 设计KVCache亲和性路由器，减少计算同时保持负载均衡；4) 多模态输入优化：GPU图像预处理、并行视频解码、多模态缓存共享。

Result: 实现了严格O(W)的SWA存储和高缓存命中率，构建了首个大规模生产级Hybrid SWA+MoE+多模态复合架构LLM服务系统。

Conclusion: 通过系统性工程优化，成功将Hybrid SWA的理论优势转化为实际生产收益，为复合架构的大规模LLM部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present a full-pipeline inference optimization for the MiMo-V2.5 model family, which combines Hybrid Sliding Window Attention (Hybrid SWA), sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), and multimodal encoders. While Hybrid SWA can ideally reduce both attention compute and KVCache storage significantly compared to Full Attention, realizing these gains in production requires substantial engineering effort. We systematically optimize the KVCache system with layerwise prefetch, SWA-aware prefix cache trees, and specialized placement strategies, achieving strict $O(W)$ SWA storage and high cache hit rates. We further build GCache, a high-performance distributed cache infrastructure with RDMA-optimized networking, and develop a KVCache-affinity router to reduce computation while preserving load balancing. We also optimize for multimodal inputs, including GPU image preprocessing, parallel video decoding, and multimodal cache sharing. Together, these optimizations constitute the first large-scale LLM serving system in production that efficiently covers the Hybrid SWA + MoE + multimodal composite architecture.

</details>


### [159] [No Attention, No Problem: DPU-Aware Attention Approximation in Modern YOLO on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13106)
*Suraj Karki,Qazi Arbab Ahmed,Thorsten Jungeblut*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对注意力YOLO变体（YOLOv26和YOLOv11）的定制化DPU感知架构，部署在AMD FPGA上，通过替换不支持的激活函数、用1×1卷积替代split操作、近似空间注意力机制等方式实现DPU兼容，在Xilinx ZCU104上对8种DPU配置进行基准测试。YOLOv26n在标准/定向检测中分别达到34.05/29.55 FPS，量化后精度平均下降5%，功耗比现有技术降低约3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI加速在实时目标检测中取得进展，但边缘设备上的目标检测需要在精度、速度和能效之间取得平衡，现有方案在这三方面尚存优化空间。

Method: 提出定制化DPU感知架构，针对注意力YOLO变体进行DPU兼容改造：替换不支持的激活函数、将split操作替换为1×1卷积、近似空间注意力机制。在Xilinx ZCU104上部署YOLOv26和YOLOv11，覆盖标准/定向目标检测任务，在8种DPU配置上完成全面评估。

Result: YOLOv26n和YOLOv26n-OBB分别实现34.05和29.55 FPS的端到端吞吐量，量化后精度平均下降约5%，功耗比现有技术低约3倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的DPU感知架构能有效在FPGA上高效部署注意力YOLO模型，在保持可接受精度损失的前提下显著提升能效和吞吐量，为边缘AI实时目标检测提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Edge-based Artificial Intelligence (AI) acceleration has recently improved progress in real-time object detection. Object detection on edge devices requires a balance between accuracy, speed, and power efficiency. This paper proposes a customized Deep Learning Processor Unit (DPU)-aware architecture for attention-based YOLO variants deployed on AMD FPGAs. Specifically, we evaluate and benchmark YOLOv26 and YOLOv11, two modern attention-based YOLO variants, on the Xilinx ZCU104 across both standard and oriented object detection tasks. We replace unsupported activation functions, substitute split operations with 1x1 convolutions, and approximate the spatial attention mechanism in a DPU-compatible way. All models are then trained and evaluated across six benchmark datasets such as COCO, Pascal VOC, KITTI, DOTA, DIOR-R, and an in-house human presence dataset, and benchmarked across all eight DPU configurations (B512 to B4096) in terms of mAP, FPS, latency, power, and resource utilization. Notably, YOLOv26n and YOLOv26n-OBB deliver the highest end-to-end throughput at 34.05 and 29.55 FPS for standard and oriented detection, respectively, with an average of 5% absolute reduction in accuracy due to quantization while achieving up to approximately 3x lower power consumption compared with the state of the art.

</details>


### [160] [A Reality Check on Quantum Optimisation: Evidence from an Industrial Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13325)
*Hila Safi,Karen Wintersperger,Oliver von Sicard,Christoph Niedermeier,Wolfgang Mauerer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究探索了量子、量子启发式和经典方法在工业作业车间调度问题变体上的表现，涵盖IBM Quantum、D-Wave量子退火器和Fujitsu数字退火器三个平台。通过针对硬件特性定制问题公式，证明了硬件-软件协同设计对解决方案质量和可扩展性的关键作用，并与精确经典求解器和MILP公式进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理单元在组合优化等特定计算问题上展现出加速潜力，但其在工业实际应用中的效用仍是开放性挑战。本研究旨在评估量子及量子启发式方法在工业调度问题上的实际可行性和性能边界。

Method: 选取工业作业车间调度问题的变体，分别在IBM Quantum、D-Wave量子退火器和Fujitsu数字退火器三种平台上实现量子、量子启发式和经典方法；根据各硬件特性定制量子/经典公式，实现硬件-软件协同设计；以精确经典求解器和MILP公式为基准，全面评估求解时间、解质量和可扩展性。

Result: 量子与量子启发式优化在工业求解器选择、经典工作流集成、建模决策以及早期概念验证开发中具有支持作用；同时指出了改进工业调度近似方法的潜在路径。

Conclusion: 硬件-软件协同设计对量子优化的实际应用至关重要；量子及量子启发式方法虽尚不能完全取代精确经典求解器，但在工业调度领域可作为有效的辅助工具，为未来改进方向提供了依据。

Abstract: Quantum Processing Units promise speed-ups for selected computational problems, including combinatorial optimisation, but their industrial utility remains an open challenge. We study an industrial variant of the Job-Shop Scheduling Problem using quantum, quantum-inspired, and classical methods across three platforms: IBM Quantum, the D-Wave Quantum Annealer, and the Fujitsu Digital Annealer. By tailoring formulations to hardware-specific constraints, we show that hardware-software co-design is essential for solution quality and scalability. We benchmark all approaches against an exact classical solver and a MILP formulation, evaluating runtime, solution quality, and scalability. Our results indicate that quantum and quantum-inspired optimisation can support industrial solver selection, integration in classical workflows, modelling decisions, and early proof-of-concept development, while suggesting a potential path towards improved approximations for industrial scheduling.

</details>


### [161] [CIMERA: Compute-in-Interconnect and Memory with Reconfigurable Precision for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13649)
*Yue Jiet Chong,Yimin Wang,Wei Zhang,Xuanyao Fong*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CIMERA是一种可重构精度的LLM推理加速器，通过计算互联与内存集成来缓解存储墙问题，相比Nvidia H100，在1B和13B模型上分别实现最高25倍和10倍的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在从数据中心到边缘设备的推理过程中面临巨大的计算和内存开销，传统精度方案难以平衡推理精度、吞吐量与能耗，而现代LLM工作负载具有显著的异构性和精度容忍度，需要自适应精度执行方案。

Method: 提出CIMERA架构，将计算与互联及内存集成（compute-in-interconnect and memory），支持可重构精度执行，从而缓解存储墙问题。

Result: 相比Nvidia H100，CIMERA在1B模型上可提升高达25倍能效，在13B模型上可提升高达10倍能效。

Conclusion: 通过可重构精度和计算-互联-内存集成设计，CIMERA能显著提升LLM推理能效，为不同规模模型的能效优化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: LLM impose significant computational and memory demands, creating challenges for energy-efficient inference across platforms ranging from data centers to power-constrained edge devices. Weight precision plays a critical role in balancing inference accuracy, throughput, and energy consumption, while modern LLM workloads exhibit pronounced heterogeneity and tolerance that favors adaptive precision execution. This paper presents CIMERA, a reconfigurable-precision LLM inference accelerator that integrates compute-in-interconnect and memory to mitigate the memory wall and enable precision-aware execution. Compared to Nvidia H100, CIMERA delivers up to $25\times$ and $10\times$ higher energy efficiency for 1B and 13B models, respectively.

</details>


### [162] [Kaleido: Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design for Video Diffusion Transformers by Exploiting Latent Space Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13770)
*Wenxuan Miao,Haosong Liu,Weiming Hu,Zihan Liu,Aiyue Chen,Jianlin Yu,Yiwu Yao,Yiming Gan,Jieru Zhao,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Kaleido是一种面向视频扩散Transformer(vDiT)的算法-硬件协同设计方法，通过利用通道级时空相关性来跳过冗余计算，在保持高生成质量的同时实现了高达5.9倍加速和16.0倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: vDiT生成高质量视频但计算成本极高，尤其是减少扩散时间步后自注意力计算成为主要瓶颈。现有加速方法继承自大语言模型的稀疏注意力技术，未能考虑视频数据独特的时空相关性。

Method: 提出轻量级通道级复用算法，通过重用部分结果跳过冗余计算；设计类脉动阵列加速器，包含可重构处理单元和轻量级数据调度器，以缓解复用算法带来的不规则稀疏性和数据访问模式。

Result: 在三个主流vDiT模型上，Kaleido相比最先进的加速器实现高达5.9倍加速和16.0倍能效提升，生成质量比此前方法高>17 dB。

Conclusion: 通过利用通道级时空相关性进行算法-硬件协同设计，可有效解决vDiT的计算瓶颈，在显著加速的同时保持高生成质量。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers (vDiTs) generate high quality video but introduce extremely high compute cost due to the long diffusion timesteps and self attention computation. As diffusion timesteps are reduced, the computation cost of self attention becomes the dominant bottleneck. Existing acceleration approaches largely inherit sparse attention techniques from large language models, which fail to consider the unique spatiotemporal correlation of video data.
  This paper presents Kaleido, an algorithm hardware codesign that accelerates all operations in vDiTs by exploiting channel-wise spatiotemporal correlations in latent space. Based on this insight, we propose a lightweight channelwise reuse algorithm that skips redundant computations by reusing partial results while preserving higher generative quality than prior methods (>17 dB). To efficiently support this algorithm, we design a systolic array like accelerator with reconfigurable processing elements and a lightweight data dispatcher to mitigate irregular sparsity and data access patterns introduced by our reuse algorithm. Evaluations across three mainstream vDiT models show that Kaleido achieves up to 5.9x speedup and 16.0x energy savings over state of the art accelerators.

</details>


### [163] [Jack of All Scales: A Versatile FPGA Tensor Block for MXFP Precisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13898)
*Marwan Mekhemer,Ahmed Elsousy,Balaji Venkatesh,Raphael Rowley,Vaughn Betz,Nachiket Kapre,Andrew Boutros*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对当前FPGA架构对新型MXFP低精度数值格式（MXFP4/6/8）的本地支持不足问题，系统评估了Altera Agilex-5 FPGA上MXFP点积实现的多种策略，发现张量模式虽对MXFP4/6 (E2M1/E2M3)密度最高，但无法支持MXFP6 (E3M2)及MXFP8。为此提出对DSP张量模式的针对性修改，实现对所有MXFP精度的原生支持并保持向后兼容，在ASAP7 PDK下评估面积开销后，优选方案使DSP tile面积增加36%（仅占FPGA总die面积的1.8%），在矩阵乘法中实现平均4.2倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习工作负载日益依赖窄数值格式以提高效率和减少内存占用，新标准化的MXFP格式族（MXFP8/6/4）为低精度推理提供了实用方案，但当前FPGA架构的DSP模块对这些格式的原生支持非常有限，需要探索高效实现方案。

Method: 首先在Altera Agilex-5 FPGA上对MXFP点积实现进行全面的特性化分析，涵盖纯软逻辑、DSP定点/浮点/张量模式等多种策略；然后基于分析发现的张量模式覆盖缺口，提出对DSP块内部张量模式架构的针对性修改，使用ASAP7 PDK实现简化版DSP核心进行面积评估，并设计多种权衡格式覆盖、算术密度和面积开销的修改方案。

Result: 张量模式对MXFP4 (E2M1)和MXFP6 (E2M3)算术密度最高，但无法实现MXFP6 (E3M2)和所有MXFP8精度。优选DSP修改方案使DSP tile面积增加36%（仅占FPGA总die面积的1.8%），在全部MXFP格式上实现平均4.2倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 通过对DSP张量模式架构的适度修改（面积开销可控），可实现对所有MXFP精度的原生硬件支持，显著提升FPGA上低精度深度学习推理的性能。

Abstract: Modern deep learning workloads increasingly rely on narrow numerical formats to improve efficiency and reduce memory footprint. The recently standardized microscaling floating-point (MXFP) family of formats, including MXFP8, MXFP6, and MXFP4, offers a practical approach to low-precision inference, yet the digital signal processing (DSP) blocks in current FPGA architectures offer limited native support for these formats. In this work, we first present a comprehensive characterization of MXFP dot product implementations on Altera Agilex-5 FPGAs, exploring a range of strategies spanning pure soft logic, DSP blocks in fixed-point, floating-point, and tensor modes. Our results show that while the tensor mode delivers the highest arithmetic density for MXFP4 (E2M1) and MXFP6 (E2M3), it cannot implement MXFP6 (E3M2) or any MXFP8 precisions, forcing designers to fall back to lower-density alternatives. Motivated by this gap, we propose targeted modifications to the DSP block's internal tensor-mode architecture that enable native support for all MXFP precisions while retaining backward compatibility. We estimate the area cost of these modifications using a simplified version of the Agilex-5 DSP block core implemented using the open-source ASAP7 PDK. We evaluate a variety of modified DSP block designs that present a tradeoff between format coverage, arithmetic density, and area overhead. Our preferred design point increases the DSP tile area by 36%, corresponding to only 1.8\% of the total FPGA die area. We evaluate the device-level impact of our enhanced DSP block by comparing systolic array matrix multiplier implementations across all MXFP precisions, contrasting the best-available strategies on the existing architecture against designs leveraging our modified DSP block. Our results demonstrate an average throughput improvement of 4.2x across all supported MXFP formats.

</details>
